Could the true optimum be below 1 and only >1 by total area? Let's test uniform-like U-shape with f(x)=exp(-x/n²?)? Maybe. Let's do continuous density f on [0,1] with mass at endpoints and width w. The sum distribution of two iid variables: components:

Potential algorithm:
```
p = init
ψ = log(p+eps)
for time:
  c,pmax; if improve store.
  w = softmax(beta*(c-cmax))
  h = 2 ifft(W*P)[:n]
  g = h - p·h (centered)
  # backtracking: for eta in [eta_init, ...]
     p_cand = p * exp(-eta*g); normalize; if objective decreases accept
  if no improvement, reduce eta, random perturb
```
This is like natural gradient. Since h has mean maybe not zero. Natural gradient can navigate simplex. Could be slower but stable.

def param_obj_grad(theta, features, beta):
    p=...

    L = maxc + (shifted.max()/beta?) Actually L=(1/β) logsumexp(β(c-cmax)) + cmax? logsumexp(βc)=βcmax + logsumexp(β(c-cmax)). L = cmax + (1/β) logsumexp(β(c-cmax)). Good.

    Fmean = features.T @ p  # m
    grad = (features.T @ (p*h)) - Hbar*Fmean
    return L, grad
```

Implementation details for param:
```
def param_objective(theta, feats, beta):
    psi = feats @ theta
    psi -= psi.max()
    f = np.exp(psi)
    p = f/f.sum()
    n=p.shape[0]; S=np.sqrt(2*n)
    a=p*S
    c=conv_self(a)
    cm=c.max()
    sh=beta*(c-cm); sh-=sh.max(); e=np.exp(sh); s=e.sum()
    if s==0: val=cm
    else: val=cm + np.log(s)/beta
    return val

def param_gradient(...): similar; compute h=grad_weights(a,w) (w over c length 2n-1). Then p_h = p*h; Fmean=feats.T@p; Q=feats.T@(p*h); Hbar=np.dot(p,h); grad=S*(Q - Hbar*Fmean)
```

```
def local_search(a, max_time, rng, init_alpha=0.2, betas=None, tol_list=None):
    n=len(a); S=sqrt(2n); p=a/S; psi=log(p) maybe but a update.
    best_c=a
    best=conv max
    alpha=0.2
    mode=0; stuck=0; last=0
    while time:
       c=conv(a); cur=c.max()
       if cur<best: best=cur; best_a=a; last=0; alpha*=1.05?
       stuck=stuck
       # weights
       if stuck > 1000: include wider tol; beta cycle
       w = choose_weights(c, stuck, rng)
       h=grad_weights(a,w); g=h - np.dot(a,h)/S
       gmax=max(abs(g)); if tiny random
       alphas = [alpha, alpha*0.5, alpha*2 if]
       best_cand=None; best_val=cur*(1+1e-12)
       for aa in alphas:
          if aa<=0 continue
          # natural exp update
          ax = a * np.exp(-aa * (g / max(gmax,1e-12)))
          if not finite or sum<=0: continue
          ax *= S/np.sum(ax)
          cx=conv(ax); mx=cx.max()
          if mx<best_val: choose
          # also try FW if random
       if best_cand:
          a=best_cand; alpha*=1.01? maybe keep
       else:
          # try small random perturbation
          noise = rng.normal(0, 0.002*S/np.sqrt(n), n) maybe; a = proj(a+noise)
          alpha*=0.8
    return best_a, best
```
But `g/gmax` makes alpha scale-free. If alpha=0.2, max log change 0.2. Good. Could be too small; try alpha up to1. For each iteration 3 FFT. We can do 1000s iterations; time? 3000 FFT for n=16384 size32768 maybe ~1s? Okay. Maybe 2000.

Let's code FISTA improved:
```
def run_fista(a_init, max_time, rng, beta_init=20, beta_rate=0.002, beta_max=1000, step_scale=1.0, active_after=2000):
    n=len; S=sqrt(2n); a=a_init*S/sum; y=a; x=a; t=1; best...
    stuck=0; it=0
    while time<max_time:
       it++
       beta = min(beta_max, beta_init*exp(beta_rate*it))
       c=conv(y); cur=c.max(); update best; stuck.
       if stuck>active_after and (it%20==0 or rng<0.1):
           w = active weights with tol 0.002, k maybe 32
       else:
           w = lse_weights(c,beta)
       grad=grad_weights(y,w)
       step = step_scale / beta
       # maybe adaptive step if norm: step = min(step, 2/(beta*np.linalg.norm? no)
       # backtracking every 1000? 
       x_new=project_simplex(y - step*grad, S)
       t_new=...
       y= x_new + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x_new-x)
       y=np.maximum(y,0); x=x_new; t=t_new
       # if y not finite or sum>0, renorm

Features standardization:
```
def make_features(n, D):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    z=x-0.5
    cols=[]
    # polynomials degree 1..min(6,D)
    deg=min(6,D)
    for d in range(1,deg+1): cols.append(z**d)
    rem=D-deg
    for m in range(1, rem+1): cols.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x))
    # If D<6, adjust
    feats=np.vstack(cols).T
    mean=feats.mean(axis=0); std=feats.std(axis=0); std=np.maximum(std,1e-12); feats=(feats-mean)/std
    return feats
```
If D=12: 6 poly +6 cos. Good.

Now, code details for `project_simplex` ensure returns sum S. Use `S` positive.
```
def project_simplex(v, S):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    n=len(v)
    if n==0 or S<=0: return np.zeros(n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)): v=np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0, posinf=0, neginf=0)
    w=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cs=np.cumsum(w)
    idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    theta=(cs-S)/idx
    mask=theta<=w
    if np.any(mask): rho=int(np.flatnonzero(mask)[-1]+1) else: rho=n
    theta=(cs[rho-1]-S)/rho
    p=np.maximum(v-theta,0)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: p=np.ones(n)/n; p*=S
    else: p*=S/s
    return p
```
If S=1 maybe.

`normalize_a(a)`:
```
a=np.clip(np.asarray(a,float),0,1000)
s=a.sum()
if s<1e-12: a=np.ones(len(a))
else: a = a * (np.sqrt(2*len(a))/s)
a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
return a
```
This changes sum to S.

Upsample:
```
def upsample(a, factor):
    n=len(a); n_new=n*factor
    x_old=np.arange(n); x_new=np.linspace(0,n-1,n_new,endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
```
Then normalize.
Downsample:
```
def downsample(a, n_target):
    if len(a)==n_target: return copy
    # Use rolling mean via FFT? simple reshape if divisible, else interp.
    if len(a) % n_target !=0 or len(a)<n_target:
       x_old=np.arange(len(a)); x_new=np.linspace(0,len(a)-1,n_target,endpoint=False); return np.interp(...)
    else: return a.reshape(n_target,-1).mean(axis=1)
```
Normalize after.

Algorithm local_search detailed:
```
def local_search(a_init, max_time, rng, setting_idx=0, alpha=0.2):
    n=len; S=sqrt(2n); a=normalize(a_init); a=np.maximum(a, 1e-10*S/n)
    a=S*a/a.sum()
    best_a=a; c=conv_self(a); best=c.max(); last=0; it=0; stuck=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    while time<max_time:
       it+=1
       cur=best # but need current a's c; we have from previous maybe
       if cur<best - 1e-14? We update after accept. At start cur=best.
       w=choose(c, setting[setting_idx])
       h=grad_weights(a,w); g=h - np.dot(a,h)/S
       gmax=np.max(np.abs(g))
       if gmax<1e-15: random; continue
       gn=g/gmax
       trials=[]
       for aa in [alpha, alpha*0.5, alpha*2, alpha*4]:
           ax=a*np.exp(-aa*gn); if finite and sum>0: ax*=S/sum; trials.append(ax)
       # FW trial if it%20==0
       if it%20==0:
           sidx=int(np.argmin(h))
           for gamma in [0.5,0.25]:
              ex=np.zeros(n); ex[sidx]=S; ax=(1-gamma)*a+gamma*ex; normalize; trials.append
       best_trial=None; best_trial_val=None
       current=c.max()
       for ax in trials:
           cx=conv_self(ax); mx=cx.max()
           if best_trial_val is None or mx<best_trial_val:
              best_trial_val=mx; best_trial=ax
       if best_trial_val < current - 1e-14:
           a=best_trial; c=conv_self(a) # could reuse candidate c? We didn't store; could store to save one FFT.
           if c.max() < best - 1e-14:
               best=float(c.max()); best_a=a.copy(); last=it; alpha=min(2.0, alpha*1.02)
           else:
               last=it # accepted not best? maybe
           stuck=0
       else:
           stuck+=1
           alpha*=0.8
           if stuck>100:
              setting_idx=(setting_idx+1)%len
           if stuck>500:
              # random reheat
              noise_scale = 0.003*S/np.sqrt(n) * max(0.2, np.exp(-stuck/1000))
              a=project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0,noise_scale,n), S)
              a=np.maximum(a,1e-10*S/n); a*=S/a.sum()
              c=conv_self(a); current=c.max(); last=it; stuck=0; alpha=0.2
    return best_a,best
```

```
def run_fista(a_init, max_time, rng, beta_init=10.0, beta_rate=0.001, beta_max=200.0, step_scale=1.0, active_mode=False):
    a=normalize; S; y=a; x=a; t=1; best...
    stuck=0; c=conv(y); best=c.max(); last_it=0; bad=0
    while time:
       beta=...
       c=conv(y); cur=c.max(); if cur<best: best...
       w = choose based on mode/stuck
       grad=grad_weights(y,w)
       step=step_scale / max(beta,1e-12)
       x_new=project_simplex(y - step*grad, S)
       t_new=...
       y_next = x_new + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x_new-x)
       y_next=np.maximum(y_next,0); y_next*=S/y_next.sum()
       # check objective of y_next? if too bad, step half
       # but to avoid extra FFT maybe check cur_next? no
       x=x_new; y=y_next; t=t_new
       if time%? periodically recompute? c at top next.
    return best
```

`conv_self(a)`:
```
def conv_self(a):
    a=np.maximum(np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64),0.0)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    m=2*n-1
    size=next_pow2(m)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, size)
    c=c[:m]
    # remove tiny negative
    if c.size:
       mn=c.min()
       if mn < 0:
          # if negative significant maybe due to input? but nonneg conv not. clip
          c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
```
Using rfft: `irfft(A*A, size)` works because A*A not Hermitian? A is rfft (positive freq); product is not necessarily Hermitian? For real convolution of a with a, A*A should be Hermitian (since full complex squared of rfft? rfft only nonnegative; product at DC/N/2 real, internal not complex? Actually rfft returns complex; A*A (elementwise) is not guaranteed Hermitian in the rfft half? But irfft expects an array representing half-spectrum with appropriate conjugate symmetry; A*A of rfft of real sequence is indeed the rfft of the convolution? Let's verify: Full FFT of a*a is FFT(a)*FFT(a). rfft returns half of full FFT. Elementwise square of half spectrum gives half of full spectrum, symmetric automatically as half of real convolution. So irfft is valid. Good.
But if using `np.fft.irfft(A*A, n=size)`, output length size. Good.

Could use full for absolute safety with gradient? For conv rfft fast. Gradient uses full? We'll implement gradient with rfft too? Cross-correlation conj(P)*W of rfft half: Is `irfft(np.conj(P)*W)` valid? `np.conj(P)*W` is half-spectrum of real cross-correlation if P,W are rffts? The cross-correlation is real; its full spectrum is conj(P_full)*W_full. Half spectrum at positive freq is conj(P_r)*W_r. So irfft works. Use rfft for speed. Need size >= n + len(w)-1. Good.
```
def grad_weights(a,w):
    n=len(a); m=len(w); size=next_pow2(n+m-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(np.maximum(a,0), size); W=np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    g=2.0*np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*W, size)[:n]
    g=np.maximum(g,? no can be negative)
    return g
```
For weights nonnegative sum1, cross-correlation nonnegative? g_i=2∑w_k a_{k-i}; nonnegative. Roundoff small negative; we can `g=np.maximum(g,0)`? Derivative of quadratic with nonnegative weights is nonnegative for a≥0, yes. Clip to 0 to avoid negative tiny. This also enforces descent? If all zero? fine. Use `g=np.real`; `g[g<0]=0`? def eval_candidate(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s < 0.01: return inf  # evaluator would but we can
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(s)): return inf
    c=conv_self(a); mx=c.max()
    if not np.isfinite(mx): return inf
    return float(2.0*n*mx/(s*s))
```

`normalize_a(a)`:
```
def normalize_a(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).copy(); a=np.clip(a,0,1000); n=a.size
    if n==0: return a
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-300 or not finite: a=np.ones(n)
    else: a = a * (np.sqrt(2.0*n)/s)
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    return a
```

def upsample(a, factor):
    n=len(a); m=n*factor
    if factor==1: return a.copy()
    x_old=np.arange(n,dtype=float); x_new=np.linspace(0.0,n-1.0,m,endpoint=False)
    b=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return b
```

def downsample(a, target):

    if n % target ==0: return a.reshape(target, n//target).mean(axis=1)

```

    a,obj=run_fista(a,t,rng,5,0.002,100,1.0)
# after run_fista, update

   a2,obj2=local_search(a,t,rng)
   if obj2<obj: a=obj
remaining...

if remaining>5:
   t=min(300, remaining)
   a2,obj2=local_search(a,t,rng)
   if obj2<obj: ...
return a,obj

def make_active_weights(c, frac):
    n=len(c); cm=max
    w=zeros
    if frac <= 1e-8:
       w[argmax]=1; return w
    threshold = cm - max(frac*cm, 1e-15)
    idx=np.flatnonzero(c >= threshold)
    if idx.size==0: idx=[argmax]
    if idx.size > 64 and idx.size < n: # if too many, reduce
       k=min(64, max(1, int(frac*n)*2? maybe))
       idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
    # But if active < 64, use all. If active >64, top 64.
    vals=c[idx] - vals.min() + 1.0
    vals=vals**2
    w[idx]=vals/vals.sum()

Function `next_pow2`:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()
```

`conv_self` uses `np.fft.rfft`. Need for gradient with real output from rfft: `irfft` of product length size. Check if product satisfies Hermitian? For cross/conv yes. To be safe, we can use full fft for gradient to avoid rfft orientation sign subtleties. But rfft should be fine. Let's maybe use full for gradient because orientation is more critical? Performance still okay (size 32768/131072; full complex ~2x memory but okay). Simpler correctness with full:
```
def grad_weights(a,w):
   n=len(a); m=len(w); size=next_pow2(n+m-1)
   A=np.fft.fft(a,size); W=np.fft.fft(w,size)
   g=2.0*np.real(np.fft.ifft(np.conj(A)*W, size))[:n]
   g=np.maximum(g,0)
   return g
```
Full ifft uses complex; okay. For n=32768 size131072, full FFT maybe 2x slower but still okay. rfft shorter. I think rfft correct. Let's prove orientation: DFT xf; for real x, `np.fft.rfft` returns X[k] for k=0..N/2. `np.fft.irfft(conj(A)*W, N)` returns sequence whose rfft is conj(A)*W. The full spectrum of y is y_full[k]=conj(A_full[k])*W_full[k]. For k=0..N/2 this equals conj(A_r[k])*W_r[k], and negative frequencies conjugate. So yes. Orientation: ifft(conj(A)*W) at index l (0..N-1) = 1/N∑ conj(A[k])W[k]e^{2πikl/N}. For l from 0 to n-1, equals ∑_{j} a_j w_{j+l}? Cross-correlation. Good. Use rfft for speed. For conv, irfft(A*A) size. Good.

`conv_self`: after irfft, due to numeric, values maybe negative tiny. Clip.

```
def simple_sweep(n, best_a, best_obj, deadline, rng):
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*n); x=...
    candidates=[]
    candidates.append(np.ones(n))
    for g in np.linspace(-3,3,13): candidates.append(np.exp(g*x))
    # reverse exp
    for g in np.linspace(-3,3,9): candidates.append(np.exp(g*(1-x)))
    for k in range(1,6): for amp in (0.3,0.6,0.85): for ph in (0, 0.5*np.pi, np.pi, 1.5*np.pi):
        f=1+amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x+ph); f=np.maximum(f,0.05); candidates.append(f)
    # random DCT coefficients small
    for _ in range(6):
       f=np.ones(n); for m in range(1,6): f += rng.normal()*0.5*np.cos(2πm x)
       f=np.maximum(f,0.05); append
    for c in candidates:
       b=np.maximum(c,1e-300); b*=S/b.sum(); obj=conv_self(b).max() # normalized
       if obj<best_obj update
    # transform best_a: reverse, add noise, random roll
    for _ in range(8):
       b=best_a.copy(); b=np.maximum(b,0); b*=S/b.sum()
       if _%4==0: b=b[::-1]
       elif _%4==1: b=np.roll(b,1)
       elif _%4==2: b=np.roll(b,n//8)
       else: b=b + rng.normal(0,0.002*S/np.sqrt(n),n)
       b=project_simplex(b,S); update
    return
```
But `best_a` length should equal n. If not, downsample. In propose we set same.

def param_search(n,best_a,best_obj,deadline,rng):
   if minimize is None: return
   configs=[(12,10.0,4,100),(20,30.0,4,100),(26,80.0,2,100)]
   best_a,best_obj=...
   for D,beta,count,maxiter in configs:
      if time.time? use time.monotonic > deadline: break
      feats=make_features(n,D)
      # generate starts count
      starts=[]
      starts.append(np.zeros(D))
      # projection
      S=np.sqrt(2*n); p=best_a/S; p=max... target=log; theta=feats.T@target; theta=clip; if norm>0 theta/=norm; starts.append(theta)
      for j in range(count-2): starts.append(rng.normal(0,1,D))
      for th in starts:
         if now>deadline-10: break
         try:
             res=minimize(param_lse_value, th, jac=param_lse_grad, args=(feats,beta), method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-8,'gtol':1e-6})
         except: pass
         if res.success? or finite:
             a=...
             obj=eval_candidate or conv max
             update
   return

Now, `make_features`: Standardize features so norm1.
```
def make_features(n,D):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    z=x-0.5
    cols=[]
    poly_deg=min(6,D)
    for d in range(1,poly_deg+1): cols.append(z**d)
    rem=D-poly_deg
    for m in range(1,rem+1): cols.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x))
    # if D<6, adjust: choose first D cos? Need robust.
```
If D=12, poly6 rem6. If D=5, poly5 rem0. D min 12 in configs.
```
    feats=np.vstack(cols).T
    # remove columns all zero? not
    mean=feats.mean(0); std=feats.std(0); std=np.maximum(std,1e-12); feats=(feats-mean)/std
    return feats
```
Standardizing cos removes mean (zero for full period if n divisible? okay). Polynomial variances.

Now, `run_fista` details:
```
def run_fista(a_init, max_time, rng, beta_init=10.0, beta_rate=0.001, beta_max=100.0, step_scale=1.0):
    t0=time.monotonic(); end=t0+max_time
    if max_time<=0: return a_init, eval_candidate...
    n=len(a_init); S=np.sqrt(2*n)
    a=normalize_a(a_init)
    y=a.copy(); x=a.copy(); t_mom=1.0
    c=conv_self(y); best=float(c.max()); best_a=a.copy(); it=0; bad=0
    while t0? while time.monotonic()<end:
       it+=1
       c=conv_self(y); cur=float(c.max())
       if cur < best - 1e-14: best=cur; best_a=y.copy(); bad=0
       else: bad+=1
       beta = min(beta_max, beta_init*(1.0+0.002*it)?) exponential: beta_init*np.exp(beta_rate*it) but for large it overflow; use min.
       if bad > 2000:
           w=make_active_weights(c,0.001)
       else:
           w=make_lse_weights(c,beta)
       grad=grad_weights(y,w)
       step=step_scale / max(beta, 1e-12)
       if bad>4000: step*=0.5; # maybe
       x_new=project_simplex(y - step*grad, S)
       t_new=0.5*(1+sqrt(1+4*t_mom*t_mom))
       y_new = x_new + ((t_mom-1)/t_new)*(x_new - x)
       y_new=np.maximum(y_new,0.0)
       sy=y_new.sum()
       if sy>0: y_new*=S/sy else y_new=project
       x=x_new; y=y_new; t_mom=t_new
       if (it%5000==0): # restart
          t_mom=1.0; y=project_simplex(y + rng.normal(0, 0.001*S/np.sqrt(n), n), S)
    return best_a,best
```
Using `bad` incremented every iteration not improving, but FISTA with LSE might not improve monotonically; after high beta it will plateau. Bad may reach 2000 and use active. Good.
But if no improve for long, restart noise every 5000 only if time. Also if `bad>10000`, maybe stop? Could break to save time. But function has max_time; we can break if bad>20000 and no chance? Perhaps break to allow other optimizers. Add:
```
if bad > 30000: break
```

Now, `local_search` code in detail:
```
def local_search(a_init, max_time, rng, start_setting=0, start_alpha=0.2):
    t0=time.monotonic(); end=t0+max_time
    n=len(a_init); S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    a=normalize_a(a_init)
    floor=1e-10*S/ n
    a=np.maximum(a,floor); a*=S/a.sum()
    c=conv_self(a); cur=float(c.max()); best=float(cur); best_a=a.copy()
    setting_idx=int(start_setting % len(LOCAL_SETTINGS)); alpha=float(start_alpha)
    stuck=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic()<end:
       it+=1
       if cur < best - 1e-14? We already update when accept; at top use cur.
       mode,param = LOCAL_SETTINGS[setting_idx]
       w = choose_weights(c, mode, param)
       h=grad_weights(a,w)
       mean = np.dot(a,h)/S
       g=h - mean
       gmax=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
       if gmax <= 1e-15:
           # random small noise
           a=project_simplex(a + rng.normal(...),S); c=conv; cur=...; update; stuck=0; setting_idx+=1; continue
       gn = g/gmax
       # determine alpha
       alphas=[]
       for aa in (alpha, alpha*0.5, alpha*2.0):
          aa=max(1e-6, min(2.0, aa))
          if aa not in alphas (approx) append
       trials=[]
       for aa in alphas:
          ax=a*np.exp(-aa*gn)
          if np.all(np.isfinite(ax)):
             ssum=ax.sum()
             if ssum>0:
                ax=ax*S/sum
                trials.append(ax)
       if it % 4 == 0:
          for eta in (0.02,0.05):
             ax=project_simplex(a - eta*h, S)
             trials.append(ax)
       # FW if it%20
       if it%20==0:
          sidx=int(np.argmin(h))
          ex=np.zeros(n); ex[sidx]=S
          for gamma in (0.25,0.5):
              ax=(1-gamma)*a + gamma*ex
              ax=project_simplex(ax,S)
              trials.append(ax)
       # choose best trial
       best_trial=None; best_trial_c=None; best_trial_val=cur*(1+1e-13)
       for ax in trials:
          cx=conv_self(ax); mx=float(cx.max())
          if mx < best_trial_val:
             best_trial_val=mx; best_trial=ax; best_trial_c=cx
       if best_trial is not None and best_trial_val < cur - 1e-14:
           a=best_trial; c=best_trial_c; cur=best_trial_val
           if cur < best - 1e-14:
               best=cur; best_a=a.copy(); stuck=0; alpha=min(2.0, alpha*1.02)
           else:
               stuck=0 # accepted but not global? Actually cur>best? shouldn't happen because cur was current >best? Could be due to previous best lower. If accepted improvement from cur but cur could be >best if a not best. Local_search only tracks best_a, and a may be worse? We always set a to accepted candidate from current a; current a might be worse than best if we did random? In reheat we don't set best; a could worse. It may improve to still above best. We can keep stuck reset if any improvement.
       else:
           stuck += 1
           alpha = max(1e-3, alpha*0.8)
           if stuck > 80:
              setting_idx = (setting_idx + 1) % len(LOCAL_SETTINGS)
           if stuck > 400:
              noise_scale=0.002*S/np.sqrt(n)
              a=project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0,noise_scale,n), S)
              floor=...; a=np.maximum(a,floor); a*=S/a.sum()
              c=conv_self(a); cur=float(c.max())
              if cur < best -? update? no, if cur<best update best.
              stuck=0; setting_idx=(setting_idx+1)%len; alpha=0.2
       # If best not improve for very long? nothing
    return best_a,best
```
Need if `current` best has a not corresponding? We maintain a separately from best. If random reheat makes a worse, later improvements from current may be above best but `best_trial_val < cur` but not `best`. We track best only if cur<best. If a is worse than best and it cannot beat best, it might still keep improving from worse; okay. We could reset a to best_a when it improves? If best > current? Actually if a worse, local_search may waste. We can after reheat or if no improve for e.g. 1000, set a=best_a.copy(); c=conv(best_a). This is good: When stuck > 1000 and no best, restart from best. Implement:
```
if stuck > 1000 and cur >= best:
    a=best_a.copy(); c=conv_self(a); cur=best; stuck=0; setting_idx=...; alpha=0.2
```
This ensures search around best.

However, if local_search starts from a and best initialized cur, a=best. It may move to worse after accepting? We accept only if candidate improves current, not necessarily best. It can wander to plateau higher than best? Starting best, first accepted may improve best, so cur=best. If later a accepted but cur still >best (if a had worsened due to random), could happen. We'll reset.

`upsample` expects factor int? For arbitrary target, make `resample(a, target)` using interpolation. We'll implement `resample(a, target)`:
```
def resample(a, target):
    n=len(a)
    if target==n: return a.copy()
    x_old=np.arange(n,dtype=float); x_new=np.linspace(0.0, max(n-1,0), target, endpoint=False)
    if target<n:
       return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    else:
       # if target up to multiple, use x_new 0..n-1; extrapolate edges to a[0],a[-1]
       b=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a, left=a[0], right=a[-1])
       return b
```

`param_p_from_theta(theta, feats)`:
```
def param_p_from_theta(theta, feats):
    theta=np.asarray(theta,dtype=np.float64)
    if theta.size==0: return np.ones(feats.shape[0])/feats.shape[0]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)):
        return np.ones(feats.shape[0])/feats.shape[0]
    theta=np.clip(theta,-50.0,50.0)
    psi=feats @ theta
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(psi)):
        return np.ones(feats.shape[0])/feats.shape[0]
    psi-=np.max(psi)
    psi=np.clip(psi,-50.0,50.0)
    f=np.exp(psi)
    s=float(f.sum())
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(feats.shape[0])/feats.shape[0]
    return f/s
```

`param_lse_value` and grad both compute p and a; repeated. Could combine? Keep separate for simplicity. In grad, if value inf return zero.

```
def param_lse_grad(theta, feats, beta):
    p=param_p_from_theta(...)
    n=p.size
    S=np.sqrt(2*n)
    a=p*S
    c=conv_self(a)
    cm=c.max()
    if not finite: return zeros
    if beta<=0: w=uniform
    else: shim; w
    h=grad_weights(a,w)
    Fmean=feats.T @ p
    Q=feats.T @ (p*h)
    Hbar=np.dot(p,h)
    grad=S*(Q-Hbar*Fmean)
    if not finite: return zeros
    return grad
```
If `feats` shape (n,D), feats.T @ (p*h) works. For D large, okay.

Let's implement:
```
def grad_weights(a,w):
   n=a.size; m=w.size
   if m < 2*n-1: w=np.concatenate([w, zeros...])? We always pass full. For safety pad.
   size=next_pow2(n+m-1)
   A=rfft(a,size); W=rfft(w,size)
   g=2.0*irfft(conj(A)*W,size)[:n]
   if g.size == n:
       diag = 2*np.arange(n)
       # if diag < len(w)
       wd = w[diag]
       g -= a*wd
   g=np.maximum(g,0.0)
   return g
```
Need ensure `w` full length in simple? In local, yes. In param, yes. In FISTA, yes.

For cross-correlation orientation, the term with lag 2i is included in crosscorr at index i? For i, crosscorr sum a_j w_{j+i}. Diagonal term j=i gives a_i w_{2i}. Coefficient 2 includes 2a_i w_{2i}; subtract a_i w_{2i} correct. Good.

This correction may slightly alter FISTA step scale. Step still 1/beta. Good.

For i=1,w at k=2: 2*a_{1}=2a1. But derivative is 2a1? Wait the squared term a1² derivative is 2a1. So the ordered-pair formula counts diagonal once but multiplier 2? The two-term formula 2∑ w_k a_{k-i} gives 2a1. So no correction needed! Our earlier thought of diagonal half was for symmetric quadratic B with 1/2 offdiag; but convolution sum has diagonal term once but derivative of square 2a_i. The formula 2∑ a_{k-i} works for diagonal too: if k-i=i, gives 2a_i. So no correction. My original was wrong. ```python
"""
Minimize 2*n*max_conv/sum(a)^2 over non-negative sequences.

Strategy:
  1. Start from height_sequence_1 if available (resampled to 8192).
  2. Cheap deterministic sweeps (exponentials, sinusoids, transformed best).
  3. Low-dimensional smooth parameter search in a softmax/FFT objective.
  4. Full-dimensional multi-method polishing:
       - FISTA with log-sum-exp smoothing
       - natural-gradient line search with active / softmax / top-k weights
       - occasional Frank-Wolfe and Euclidean projected-gradient trials
  5. Multi-scale upsample to 16384 and polish if beneficial.
  6. Final direct line search and verified return.

The objective is scale-invariant.  The code normalizes to sum(a)=sqrt(2n),
in which case the value equals max(self-convolution).
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
except Exception:
    minimize = None


LOCAL_SETTINGS = (
    ("exact", 1.0),
    ("lse", 5.0),
    ("active", 0.002),
    ("topk", 8),
    ("active", 0.01),
    ("lse", 20.0),
    ("topk", 32),
    ("lse", 50.0),
    ("active", 0.05),
    ("topk", 128),
)


def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def conv_self(a):
    """Full linear self-convolution via FFT for nonnegative real a."""
    a = np.maximum(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    size = next_pow2(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, n=size)[:m]
    if c.size and c.min() < 0.0:
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def grad_weights(a, w):
    """
    Gradient wrt a of sum_k w_k (a*a)_k.
    This equals 2 * cross-correlation(a, w) at lags 0..n-1.
    """
    a = np.maximum(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    m = w.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if m < 2 * n - 1:
        pad = np.zeros(2 * n - 1 - m, dtype=np.float64)
        w = np.concatenate((w, pad))
    size = next_pow2(n + m - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    g = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A) * W, n=size)[:n]
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    return g


def eval_candidate(a):
    """Exact objective (same as evaluate_sequence, ignoring input type checks)."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    c = conv_self(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mx = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))


def normalize_a(a, target_n=None):
    """Clip to valid range and rescale to sum sqrt(2n)."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if target_n is not None and target_n != a.size:
        a = resample(a, int(target_n))
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)
    a = a * (np.sqrt(2.0 * n) / s)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a


def resample(a, target):
    """Linear interpolation resampling to target length."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    target = int(target)
    if n == 0 or target <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if target == n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, max(n - 1, 0), target, endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a, left=a[0], right=a[len(a) - 1])


def project_simplex(v, S):
    """Project onto {x >= 0, sum(x)=S}."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if S <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cs = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = (cs - S) / idx
    valid = theta <= w
    if np.any(valid):
        rho = int(np.flatnonzero(valid)[-1] + 1)
    else:
        rho = n
    theta = (cs[rho - 1] - S) / rho
    p = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    else:
        p = p * (S / s)
    return p


def make_lse_weights(c, beta):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    cm = float(c.max())
    if beta <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / c.size
    sh = beta * (c - cm)
    sh -= float(sh.max())
    e = np.exp(sh)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / c.size
    return e / s


def make_active_weights(c, frac):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = c.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    cm = float(c.max())
    w = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if frac is None or frac <= 1e-12:
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    threshold = cm - max(float(frac) * cm, 1e-15)
    idx = np.flatnonzero(c >= threshold)
    if idx.size == 0:
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    if idx.size > 256:
        k = min(256, n)
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
    vals = c[idx]
    vals = vals - float(vals.min()) + 1.0
    vals = np.maximum(vals, 1e-30) ** 2
    vals_sum = float(vals.sum())
    if vals_sum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(vals_sum):
        vals = np.ones(idx.size, dtype=np.float64)
        vals_sum = float(vals.sum())
    w[idx] = vals / vals_sum
    return w


def make_topk_weights(c, k):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = c.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    k = int(max(1, min(k, n)))
    w = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if k == 1:
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
    w[idx] = 1.0 / k
    return w


def choose_weights(c, mode, param):
    if mode == "exact":
        return make_topk_weights(c, 1)
    if mode == "lse":
        return make_lse_weights(c, float(param))
    if mode == "active":
        return make_active_weights(c, float(param))
    if mode == "topk":
        return make_topk_weights(c, int(param))
    return make_lse_weights(c, 20.0)


def run_fista(a_init, max_time, rng,
              beta_init=10.0, beta_rate=0.0015, beta_max=120.0,
              step_scale=1.0):
    """FISTA on smoothed log-sum-exp objective, normalized sum sqrt(2n)."""
    a_init = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a_init.size
    if n == 0 or max_time <= 0.0:
        return a_init, float(eval_candidate(a_init))

    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    a = normalize_a(a_init)
    y = a.copy()
    x = a.copy()
    t_mom = 1.0
    c = conv_self(y)
    best = float(c.max())
    best_a = y.copy()
    stuck = 0
    it = 0

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    end = t0 + float(max_time)

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        it += 1
        c = conv_self(y)
        cur = float(c.max())
        if cur < best - 1e-14:
            best = cur
            best_a = y.copy()
            stuck = 0
        else:
            stuck += 1

        beta = float(min(beta_max, max(1.0, beta_init * np.exp(beta_rate * it))))

        if stuck > 2500:
            w = make_active_weights(c, 0.002)
        elif stuck > 1000 and (it % 7 == 0 or float(rng.random()) < 0.15):
            w = make_topk_weights(c, 24)
        else:
            w = make_lse_weights(c, beta)

        g = grad_weights(y, w)
        step = float(step_scale) / max(beta, 1e-12)
        if stuck > 6000:
            step *= 0.5

        x_new = project_simplex(y - step * g, S)
        t_new = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t_mom * t_mom))
        y_new = x_new + ((t_mom - 1.0) / t_new) * (x_new - x)
        y_new = np.maximum(y_new, 0.0)
        sy = float(y_new.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(sy) or sy <= 1e-300:
            y_new = project_simplex(x_new, S)
        else:
            y_new = y_new * (S / sy)

        x = x_new
        y = y_new
        t_mom = t_new

        if it % 8000 == 0:
            t_mom = 1.0
            noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.001 * S / np.sqrt(float(n)), n)
            y = project_simplex(y + noise, S)

        if stuck > 25000:
            break

    return best_a, best


def local_search(a_init, max_time, rng, start_setting=0, start_alpha=0.2):
    """
    Natural-gradient line search directly minimizing the max convolution.
    Tries multiplicative updates, projected-gradient, and Frank-Wolfe trials.
    """
    a_init = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a_init.size
    if n == 0 or max_time <= 0.0:
        return a_init, float(eval_candidate(a_init))

    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    a = normalize_a(a_init)
    floor = 1e-10 * S / float(n)
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    a = a * (S / max(float(a.sum()), 1e-300))

    c = conv_self(a)
    cur = float(c.max())
    best = cur
    best_a = a.copy()
    setting_idx = int(start_setting) % len(LOCAL_SETTINGS)
    alpha = float(start_alpha)
    stuck = 0
    it = 0

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    end = t0 + float(max_time)

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        it += 1

        if cur < best - 1e-14:
            best = cur
            best_a = a.copy()

        mode, param = LOCAL_SETTINGS[setting_idx]
        w = choose_weights(c, mode, param)
        h = grad_weights(a, w)
        mean_h = float(np.dot(a, h)) / S
        g = h - mean_h
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))

        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 1e-15:
            noise_scale = 0.001 * S / np.sqrt(float(n))
            a = project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0.0, noise_scale, n), S)
            a = np.maximum(a, floor)
            a = a * (S / max(float(a.sum()), 1e-300))
            c = conv_self(a)
            cur = float(c.max())
            if cur < best - 1e-14:
                best = cur
                best_a = a.copy()
            stuck = 0
            setting_idx = (setting_idx + 1) % len(LOCAL_SETTINGS)
            continue

        gn = g / gmax
        alphas = []
        for aa in (alpha, 0.5 * alpha, 2.0 * alpha):
            aa = float(min(2.0, max(1e-4, aa)))
            if len([x for x in alphas if abs(x - aa) < 1e-8]) == 0:
                alphas.append(aa)

        trials = []
        for aa in alphas:
            ax = a * np.exp(-aa * gn)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(ax)):
                ssum = float(ax.sum())
                if ssum > 1e-300:
                    ax = ax * (S / ssum)
                    trials.append(ax)

        if it % 4 == 0:
            for eta in (0.02, 0.05):
                ax = project_simplex(a - eta * h, S)
                trials.append(ax)

        if it % 20 == 0:
            sidx = int(np.argmin(h))
            ex = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            ex[sidx] = S
            for gamma in (0.25, 0.5):
                ax = project_simplex((1.0 - gamma) * a + gamma * ex, S)
                trials.append(ax)

        best_trial = None
        best_trial_c = None
        best_trial_val = cur * (1.0 + 1e-13)

        for ax in trials:
            cx = conv_self(ax)
            mx = float(cx.max())
            if mx < best_trial_val:
                best_trial_val = mx
                best_trial = ax
                best_trial_c = cx

        if best_trial is not None and best_trial_val < cur - 1e-12:
            a = best_trial
            c = best_trial_c
            cur = best_trial_val
            if cur < best - 1e-14:
                best = cur
                best_a = a.copy()
                stuck = 0
                alpha = float(min(2.0, alpha * 1.03))
            else:
                stuck = 0
        else:
            stuck += 1
            alpha = float(max(1e-3, alpha * 0.8))
            if stuck > 80:
                setting_idx = (setting_idx + 1) % len(LOCAL_SETTINGS)
            if stuck > 400:
                noise_scale = 0.0015 * S / np.sqrt(float(n))
                a = project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0.0, noise_scale, n), S)
                a = np.maximum(a, floor)
                a = a * (S / max(float(a.sum()), 1e-300))
                c = conv_self(a)
                cur = float(c.max())
                if cur < best - 1e-14:
                    best = cur
                    best_a = a.copy()
                stuck = 0
                setting_idx = (setting_idx + 1) % len(LOCAL_SETTINGS)
                alpha = 0.2
            if stuck > 1200 and cur >= best:
                a = best_a.copy()
                c = conv_self(a)
                cur = float(c.max())
                stuck = 0
                setting_idx = (setting_idx + 1) % len(LOCAL_SETTINGS)
                alpha = 0.2

    return best_a, best


def improve_candidate(a_init, max_time, rng):
    """Mixed polishing pipeline on one candidate."""
    a_init = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64)
    if a_init.size == 0:
        return a_init, float("inf")

    a = normalize_a(a_init)
    obj = float(eval_candidate(a))
    start = time.monotonic()
    end = start + float(max_time)

    rem = max(0.0, end - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 30.0:
        t1 = min(220.0, rem * 0.35)
        a2, obj2 = run_fista(a, t1, rng,
                             beta_init=5.0, beta_rate=0.0018, beta_max=80.0,
                             step_scale=1.0)
        if np.isfinite(obj2) and obj2 < obj - 1e-12:
            a = a2
            obj = obj2

    rem = max(0.0, end - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 20.0:
        t2 = min(320.0, rem * 0.45)
        a2, obj2 = local_search(a, t2, rng, start_setting=0, start_alpha=0.2)
        if np.isfinite(obj2) and obj2 < obj - 1e-12:
            a = a2
            obj = obj2

    rem = max(0.0, end - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 20.0:
        t3 = min(200.0, rem * 0.35)
        a2, obj2 = run_fista(a, t3, rng,
                             beta_init=40.0, beta_rate=0.0008, beta_max=160.0,
                             step_scale=0.7)
        if np.isfinite(obj2) and obj2 < obj - 1e-12:
            a = a2
            obj = obj2

    rem = max(0.0, end - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 10.0:
        t4 = min(320.0, rem * 0.85)
        a2, obj2 = local_search(a, t4, rng, start_setting=4, start_alpha=0.12)
        if np.isfinite(obj2) and obj2 < obj - 1e-12:
            a = a2
            obj = obj2

    return a, obj


def simple_sweep(n, best_a, best_obj, deadline, rng):
    """Quick low-dimensional family sweeps and transformations of best_a."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return best_a, best_obj
    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64)
    if best_a.size != n:
        best_a = normalize_a(best_a, n)

    def consider(b):
        nonlocal_best_obj = best_obj  # Python scoping for nested helper not top-level
        return

    candidates = []
    candidates.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    for gamma in np.linspace(-3.0, 3.0, 13):
        candidates.append(np.exp(gamma * x))
    for gamma in np.linspace(-3.0, 3.0, 9):
        candidates.append(np.exp(gamma * (1.0 - x)))

    for k in range(1, 6):
        for amp in (0.3, 0.6, 0.85):
            for ph in (0.0, 0.5 * np.pi, np.pi, 1.5 * np.pi):
                f = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + ph)
                candidates.append(np.maximum(f, 0.05))

    for _ in range(8):
        f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for m in range(1, 6):
            f = f + float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.7)) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x)
            f = f + float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.4)) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x)
        candidates.append(np.maximum(f, 0.05))

    for b in candidates:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        b = np.maximum(np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64), 1e-300)
        b = b * (S / max(float(b.sum()), 1e-300))
        obj = float(conv_self(b).max())
        if np.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_a = b.copy()
            best_obj = obj

    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        transforms = [
            (lambda b: b[::-1]),
            (lambda b: np.roll(b, 1)),
            (lambda b: np.roll(b, max(1, n // 8))),
            (lambda b: project_simplex(b + rng.normal(0.0, 0.002 * S / np.sqrt(float(n)), n), S)),
            (lambda b: project_simplex(b + rng.normal(0.0, 0.005 * S / np.sqrt(float(n)), n), S)),
            (lambda b: project_simplex(b + np.exp(-((np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) - 0.25 * n) ** 2) / (2.0 * (0.08 * n) ** 2)), S)),
        ]
        for tf in transforms:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            b = normalize_a(tf(best_a))
            obj = float(conv_self(b).max())
            if np.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
                best_a = b.copy()
                best_obj = obj

    del consider
    return best_a, best_obj


def make_features(n, D):
    """Standardized low-frequency features for softmax log-density."""
    n = int(n)
    D = int(D)
    if n <= 0 or D <= 0:
        return np.array([[]], dtype=np.float64)
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    z = x - 0.5
    cols = []

    poly_deg = min(6, D)
    for d in range(1, poly_deg + 1):
        cols.append(z ** d)

    rem = D - poly_deg
    for m in range(1, rem + 1):
        cols.append(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x))

    if len(cols) < D:
        m = 1
        while len(cols) < D:
            cols.append(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x))
            m += 1

    feats = np.vstack(cols).T
    mu = feats.mean(axis=0)
    sd = feats.std(axis=0)
    sd = np.maximum(sd, 1e-12)
    feats = (feats - mu) / sd
    return feats


def param_p_from_theta(theta, feats):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    n = feats.shape[0]
    if theta.size == 0 or n == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n)
    theta = np.clip(theta, -50.0, 50.0)
    psi = feats @ theta
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(psi)):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n)
    psi -= float(psi.max())
    psi = np.clip(psi, -50.0, 50.0)
    f = np.exp(psi)
    s = float(f.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n)
    return f / s


def param_lse_value(theta, feats, beta):
    p = param_p_from_theta(theta, feats)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    a = p * S
    c = conv_self(a)
    cm = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cm):
        return float("inf")
    if beta <= 0.0:
        return cm
    sh = beta * (c - cm)
    sh -= float(sh.max())
    e = np.exp(sh)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return cm
    return float(cm + np.log(s) / beta)


def param_lse_grad(theta, feats, beta):
    p = param_p_from_theta(theta, feats)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0 or feats.shape[1] == 0:
        return np.zeros(feats.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    a = p * S
    c = conv_self(a)
    cm = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cm):
        return np.zeros(feats.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
    w = make_lse_weights(c, beta)
    h = grad_weights(a, w)
    Fmean = feats.T @ p
    Q = feats.T @ (p * h)
    Hbar = float(np.dot(p, h))
    grad = S * (Q - Hbar * Fmean)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return np.zeros(feats.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
    return grad


def param_search(n, best_a, best_obj, deadline, rng):
    """Smooth low-dimensional search over softmax log-densities."""
    if minimize is None:
        return best_a, best_obj
    n = int(n)
    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64)
    if best_a.size != n:
        best_a = normalize_a(best_a, n)

    configs = [
        (12, 10.0, 4, 110),
        (20, 30.0, 4, 110),
        (26, 80.0, 2, 100),
    ]
    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))

    for D, beta, nstarts, maxiter in configs:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 15.0:
            break
        try:
            feats = make_features(n, D)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if feats.size == 0:
            continue

        starts = []
        starts.append(np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64))

        p = best_a / S
        p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
        target = np.log(p)
        theta_proj = feats.T @ target
        theta_proj = np.clip(theta_proj, -20.0, 20.0)
        norm = float(np.linalg.norm(theta_proj))
        if np.isfinite(norm) and norm > 1e-12:
            theta_proj = theta_proj * (2.0 / norm)
        starts.append(theta_proj)

        for _ in range(max(0, nstarts - 2)):
            starts.append(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, D).astype(np.float64))

        for th0 in starts:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 5.0:
                break
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    param_lse_value,
                    th0,
                    jac=param_lse_grad,
                    args=(feats, beta),
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    options={
                        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                        "ftol": 1e-8,
                        "gtol": 1e-6,
                        "maxls": 20
                    }
                )
            except Exception:
                continue
            try:
                th1 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(th1)):
                    continue
                p1 = param_p_from_theta(th1, feats)
                a1 = p1 * S
                obj1 = float(eval_candidate(a1))
                if np.isfinite(obj1) and obj1 < best_obj - 1e-12:
                    best_a = normalize_a(a1)
                    best_obj = obj1
                    print("  param improved: obj=%.7f (D=%d beta=%.1f)" % (best_obj, D, beta))
            except Exception:
                pass

    return best_a, best_obj


def uniform_sequence(n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    return np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)


def get_initial_sequence(n_base):
    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        raw = None

    if raw is not None:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            if arr.size > 0:
                arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
                if arr.sum() > 1e-12:
                    if arr.size != n_base:
                        arr = resample(arr, n_base)
                    return normalize_a(arr)
        except Exception:
            pass

    return uniform_sequence(n_base)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing evaluate_sequence."""
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t_start + max(10.0, budget - 70.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    n_base = 8192
    best_a = get_initial_sequence(n_base)
    best_obj = float(eval_candidate(best_a))
    print("initial n=%d obj=%.7f" % (best_a.size, best_obj))

    # 1) cheap sweeps
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
        sweep_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 60.0)
        best_a, best_obj = simple_sweep(n_base, best_a, best_obj, sweep_deadline, rng)
        print("after sweep obj=%.7f" % best_obj)

    # 2) low-dimensional global polish
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 200.0:
        palite_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 180.0, (deadline - t_start) * 0.20)
        if palite_deadline > time.monotonic() + 20.0:
            best_a, best_obj = param_search(n_base, best_a, best_obj, palite_deadline, rng)
            print("after param obj=%.7f" % best_obj)

    # 3) full polishing at base size
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 250.0:
        remain = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
        t_base = min(700.0, max(120.0, remain * 0.38))
        a2, obj2 = improve_candidate(best_a, t_base, rng)
        if np.isfinite(obj2) and obj2 < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_a = a2
            best_obj = obj2
            print("after base polish obj=%.7f" % best_obj)

    # 4) upsample to 16384 and polish if still worthwhile
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 450.0 and best_a.size < 16384:
        a_hi = normalize_a(resample(best_a, 16384))
        obj_hi = float(eval_candidate(a_hi))
        if np.isfinite(obj_hi):
            print("upsampled 16384 pre obj=%.7f" % obj_hi)
            if obj_hi < best_obj - 1e-12:
                best_a = a_hi
                best_obj = obj_hi
        remain = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
        t_hi = min(900.0, max(80.0, remain * 0.58))
        a3, obj3 = improve_candidate(a_hi, t_hi, rng)
        if np.isfinite(obj3) and obj3 < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_a = a3
            best_obj = obj3
            print("after 16384 polish obj=%.7f" % best_obj)

    # 5) final direct local search on the current best
    remain = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if remain > 15.0 and best_a.size > 0:
        t_fin = min(260.0, max(10.0, remain * 0.45))
        a4, obj4 = local_search(best_a, t_fin, rng, start_setting=3, start_alpha=0.10)
        if np.isfinite(obj4) and obj4 < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_a = a4
            best_obj = obj4
            print("after final local obj=%.7f" % best_obj)

    # Final safety / validation.
    try:
        final_arr = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64)
        if final_arr.size == 0:
            final_arr = uniform_sequence(8192)
        final_arr = np.clip(final_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        # Ensure evaluator's sum > 0.01.
        if final_arr.sum() < 0.01:
            final_arr = uniform_sequence(8192)
        final_list = [float(v) for v in final_arr]
        try:
            verified = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
            if not np.isfinite(verified):
                raise ZeroDivisionError
            print("verified final n=%d obj=%.7f" % (len(final_list), verified))
        except Exception:
            # If our best somehow fails the official checker, return a safe uniform sequence.
            final_arr = uniform_sequence(8192)
            final_list = [float(v) for v in final_arr]
            print("fallback uniform n=%d" % final_arr.size)
    except Exception:
        final_arr = uniform_sequence(8192)
        final_list = [float(v) for v in final_arr]

    total = time.monotonic() - t_start
    print("total search time %.0f s" % total)
    return final_list
```
